# Household hh_s3 — 32 days, Monday to Thursday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: flatmates. Rooms: balcony, bathroom, bedroom_1, bedroom_2, bedroom_3, entry, kitchen, living, storage.

Residents:

- **Felix** (resident_1): works an afternoon-to-night shift; untidy (tidiness 0.39); punctual (jitter scale 0.78); rarely forgets pocket items; mood barely affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.23); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: movies, plants. Chores they take on: fixing, ironing, vacuuming.
- **Aisha** (resident_2): works from home; untidy (tidiness 0.33); erratic timing (jitter scale 1.57); rarely forgets pocket items; mood moderately affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.48); sleeps in bedroom_2, works at the bedroom_2 desk. Hobbies: friends, meditation, movies. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run, ironing, vacuuming.
- **Ines** (resident_3): works from home; very tidy (tidiness 0.92); average timing (jitter scale 1.22); sometimes forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.86); sleeps in bedroom_3, works at the bedroom_3 desk. ## Day 11 — Friday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d11`: Aisha hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:36–16:52).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d11`: Felix is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (14:57–15:27).
- `ep:rough_day_at_work:resident_1:d11`: Felix had a rough day at work: goes quiet in the evening, keeps the phone close, the jacket lands on the couch (17:30–24:00).
- `ep:sore_back:resident_1:d11`: Felix has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Aisha has low energy (0.15): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

Internal states (0–1): Felix energy 0.73, hurriedness 0.37, distraction 0.58; Aisha energy 0.15, hurriedness 0.42, distraction 0.48; Ines energy 0.29, hurriedness 0.49, distraction 0.45
